2025-06-18T11:30:03.0640085Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0640230Z <pr_title>
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0640782Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0641542Z </pr_title>
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0641735Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0641908Z <pr_description>
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0643251Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0645040Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0645172Z ## Problem
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0645540Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0646018Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0646704Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0647029Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0647373Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0647696Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0647941Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0648065Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0648869Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0649438Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0649516Z ## Solution
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0649628Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0649723Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0650050Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0650305Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0650688Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0651519Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0652168Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0652467Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0652571Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0652860Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0653054Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0653146Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0653367Z ```bash
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0654125Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0654704Z ```
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0654807Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0654899Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0655133Z ```bash
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0655920Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0656680Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0656916Z ```
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0657010Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0657170Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0657431Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0657525Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0658122Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0658643Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0658720Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0658975Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0659472Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0659990Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0660473Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0661046Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0661361Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0661741Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0662239Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0662314Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0662426Z 
2025-06-18T11:30:03.0662429Z

2025-06-18T11:30:27.7651136Z     109.## Key Components Explained
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7651381Z     110.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7651746Z     111.### 1. Pre-built ARM Wheels
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7651988Z     112.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7652280Z     113.The installation uses specially built ARMv7l-compatible wheels:
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7652635Z     114.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7653085Z     115.- **pendulum-3.1.0**: Solves Rust compilation issues that blocked Prefect installation
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7653815Z     116.- **ujson-5.10.0**: Custom fallback wheel for JSON processing on ARM
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7654293Z     117.- **prefect-3.3.4**: Full Prefect package with flow/task decorator support
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7654661Z     118.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7654852Z     119.### 2. Environment Configuration
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7655113Z     120.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7655371Z     121.The OT-2 requires specific PATH and PYTHONPATH settings:
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7655790Z     122.- Packages install to `/var/user-packages/root/.local/`
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7656146Z     123.- CLI tools need to be added to PATH
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7656473Z     124.- Python modules need PYTHONPATH configuration
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7656775Z     125.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7656965Z     126.### 3. Dependency Resolution
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7657211Z     127.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7657906Z     128.Force-reinstalling core wheels with `--no-deps` prevents pip from attempting to compile dependencies that fail on the OT-2's limited environment.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7658554Z     129.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7658734Z     130.## Usage Examples
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7658949Z     131.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7659133Z     132.### Basic Flow Example
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7659360Z     133.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7659530Z     134.```python
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7659746Z     135.from prefect import flow, task
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7660002Z     136.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7660176Z     137.@task
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7660376Z     138.def prepare_protocol():
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7660637Z     139.    print("Initializing OT-2...")
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7660911Z     140.    return "ready"
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7661122Z     141.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7661293Z     142.@task  
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7661500Z     143.def run_experiment(status):
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7661816Z     144.    print(f"Running experiment with status: {status}")
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7662153Z     145.    return "completed"
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7662380Z     146.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7662550Z     147.@flow
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7662747Z     148.def ot2_workflow():
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7662990Z     149.    status = prepare_protocol()
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7663280Z     150.    result = run_experiment(status)
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7663841Z     151.    print(f"Workflow finished: {result}")
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7664135Z     152.    return result
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7664344Z     153.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7664523Z     154.# Run the workflow
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7664752Z     155.if __name__ == "__main__":
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7664996Z     156.    ot2_workflow()
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7665207Z     157.```
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7665378Z     158.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7665555Z     159.### Flow Serving
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7665770Z     160.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7665957Z     161.Create a file `my_flow.py`:

2025-06-18T11:30:27.7667950Z     170.@flow

2025-06-18T11:30:27.7668487Z     172.    result = process_samples(sample_count)
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7668776Z     173.    return result
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7668989Z     174.

2025-06-18T11:30:27.7670080Z     178.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7670261Z     179.Run the server:
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7670472Z     180.```bash

2025-06-18T11:30:27.7671066Z     183.

2025-06-18T11:30:27.7688035Z     251.## What This Enables
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7688257Z     252.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7688464Z     253.After successful installation, you can:
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7688948Z     254.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7689348Z     255.✅ **Create and run Prefect flows** with `@flow` and `@task` decorators  
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7689879Z     256.✅ **Serve flows** using `flow.serve()` for remote execution  
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7690315Z     257.✅ **Use Prefect CLI** for workflow management  
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7690882Z     258.✅ **Connect to Prefect Cloud** for monitoring and orchestration  
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7691419Z     259.✅ **Build complex OT-2 automation workflows** with task dependencies  
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7691790Z     260.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7691978Z     261.## Architecture Notes
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7692208Z     262.
2025-06-18T11:30:27.7692483Z     263.- **Environment**: Debian-based OT-2 firmware with Python 3.10

2025-06-18T11:32:34.0561526Z       
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0562015Z       The ujson wheel is a special case - it's a minimal fallback implementation that provides the ujson interface without C extensions:

2025-06-18T11:32:34.0563074Z

2025-06-18T11:32:34.0564900Z       """
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0565207Z       Minimal ujson fallback for ARM environments where compilation fails.
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0565652Z       Provides ujson interface using standard library json.
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0565967Z       """
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0566147Z       import json
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0566337Z       
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0566559Z       # Expose standard json functions with ujson names
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0566866Z       dumps = json.dumps
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0567091Z       loads = json.loads
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0567311Z       dump = json.dump
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0567528Z       load = json.load
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0567728Z       
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0567924Z       # ujson-specific interface
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0568187Z       def encode(obj, **kwargs):
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0568449Z           return json.dumps(obj, **kwargs)
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0568712Z       
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0568901Z       def decode(s, **kwargs):
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0569149Z           return json.loads(s, **kwargs)
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0569407Z       
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0569591Z       __version__ = "5.10.0"
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0569812Z       EOF
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0569982Z       
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0570169Z       cat > setup.py << 'EOF'
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0570422Z       from setuptools import setup
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0570667Z       
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0570837Z       setup(
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0571025Z           name="ujson",
2025-06-18T11:32:34.0571249Z           version="5.10.0",

2025-06-18T11:34:04.9327092Z   +# Essential Wheel Build Instructions
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9327367Z   +

2025-06-18T11:34:04.9328598Z   +## Overview

2025-06-18T11:34:04.9328968Z   +The three essential wheels are:
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9329408Z   +- `pendulum-3.1.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl` - Date/time library (cross-compiled)
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9330039Z   +- `ujson-5.10.0-py3-none-linux_armv7l.whl` - JSON encoder/decoder (fallback implementation)

2025-06-18T11:34:04.9331395Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9331602Z   +- Docker for cross-compilation environment
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9332066Z   +- Python 3.10+
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9332334Z   +- Access to the build tools in this repository
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9332690Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9332897Z   +## Build Environment Setup
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9333185Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9333520Z   +### 1. Cross-Compilation Environment
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9334010Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9334346Z   +The repository includes a cross-compilation toolchain for ARMv7l builds:
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9334787Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9334978Z   +```bash
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9335235Z   +# Clone the repository
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9335627Z   +git clone https://github.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages.git
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9336074Z   +cd opentrons-python-packages
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9336334Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9336546Z   +# Build the cross-compilation container
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9336868Z   +cd tools
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9337088Z   +python3 -m builder.container build
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9337392Z   +```
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9337570Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9337767Z   +## Building Individual Wheels
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9338055Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9338264Z   +### 1. Pendulum Wheel (Cross-compiled)
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9338574Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9338849Z   +Pendulum requires Rust compilation for ARMv7l architecture:
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9339240Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9339418Z   +```bash
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9339623Z   +# Use the existing build script
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9339971Z   +python3 build-packages packages/pendulum/3.1.0
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9340292Z   +```
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9340469Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9340667Z   +**Alternative manual build:**
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9340955Z   +```bash
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9341176Z   +# Set up cross-compilation environment
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9341521Z   +export TARGET_ARCH=armv7l
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9341928Z   +export CARGO_TARGET_ARMV7_UNKNOWN_LINUX_GNUEABIHF_LINKER=arm-linux-gnueabihf-gcc
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9342384Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9342577Z   +# Clone pendulum source
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9342916Z   +git clone https://github.com/sdispater/pendulum.git
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9343280Z   +cd pendulum
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9343750Z   +git checkout 3.1.0
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9343969Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9344170Z   +# Install Rust and add ARM target
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9344592Z   +curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -sSf https://sh.rustup.rs | sh
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9345057Z   +rustup target add armv7-unknown-linux-gnueabihf
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9345397Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9345591Z   +# Build wheel with maturin
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9345875Z   +pip install maturin
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9346206Z   +maturin build --target armv7-unknown-linux-gnueabihf --release
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9346597Z   +```
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9346776Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9347175Z   +### 2. Prefect Wheel (Pure Python)
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9347474Z   +

2025-06-18T11:34:04.9348577Z   +# Use the existing build script

2025-06-18T11:34:04.9350260Z   +# Clone prefect source

2025-06-18T11:34:04.9352594Z   +### 3. ujson Wheel (Fallback Implementation)
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9352917Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9353628Z   +The ujson wheel is a special case - it's a minimal fallback implementation that provides the ujson interface without C extensions:
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9354325Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9354554Z   +#### Option A: Create Minimal ujson Fallback
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9354833Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9355044Z   +```bash
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9355261Z   +# Create minimal ujson implementation
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9355599Z   +mkdir ujson_fallback
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9355828Z   +cd ujson_fallback
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9356069Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9356272Z   +cat > ujson.py << 'EOF'
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9356496Z   +"""
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9356791Z   +Minimal ujson fallback for ARM environments where compilation fails.
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9357230Z   +Provides ujson interface using standard library json.
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9357538Z   +"""
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9357720Z   +import json
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9357906Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9358217Z   +# Expose standard json functions with ujson names
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9358576Z   +dumps = json.dumps
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9358798Z   +loads = json.loads
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9359032Z   +dump = json.dump
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9359250Z   +load = json.load
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9359484Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9359683Z   +# ujson-specific interface
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9359981Z   +def encode(obj, **kwargs):
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9360245Z   +    return json.dumps(obj, **kwargs)
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9360547Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9360738Z   +def decode(s, **kwargs):
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9361005Z   +    return json.loads(s, **kwargs)
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9361277Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9361463Z   +__version__ = "5.10.0"
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9361784Z   +EOF
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9362135Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9362442Z   +cat > setup.py << 'EOF'
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9362822Z   +from setuptools import setup
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9363214Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9363675Z   +setup(
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9363981Z   +    name="ujson",
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9364349Z   +    version="5.10.0",
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9364751Z   +    py_modules=["ujson"],
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9365267Z   +    description="Minimal ujson fallback for ARM",
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9365967Z   +    python_requires=">=3.7",
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9366451Z   +)
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9366795Z   +EOF
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9367094Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9367459Z   +# Build the wheel
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9368007Z   +python3 setup.py bdist_wheel --plat-name linux_armv7l
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9368629Z   +```
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9368920Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9369357Z   +#### Option B: Cross-compile ujson (Advanced)
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9369804Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9369990Z   +```bash
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9370297Z   +# This requires setting up cross-compilation toolchain
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9370698Z   +export CC=arm-linux-gnueabihf-gcc
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9371019Z   +export CXX=arm-linux-gnueabihf-g++
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9371520Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9371791Z   +git clone https://github.com/ultrajson/ultrajson.git
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9372163Z   +cd ultrajson
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9372380Z   +git checkout v5.10.0
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9372619Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9372837Z   +# Install cross-compilation dependencies
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9373618Z   +sudo apt-get install gcc-arm-linux-gnueabihf g++-arm-linux-gnueabihf
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9374197Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9374388Z   +# Build wheel
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9374726Z   +python3 setup.py bdist_wheel --plat-name linux_armv7l
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9375091Z   +```
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9375277Z   +
2025-06-18T11:34:04.9375478Z   +## Automated Build Process
